#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");

}

        }

    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    std::vector<double> C(N * N);

    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

The original loop reads a column of B with a large stride, which limits cache performance. I’ll use cache tiles and give each OpenMP worker separate output tiles, with SIMD across columns. Each result will still accumulate products in the original order.

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+            const double value = a[r * N + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                sums[r][j] += value * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+
 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
+    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
+    const double* a = A.data();
+    const double* b = B.data();
+    double* c = C.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];
+
+        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
+            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
+            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;
+
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+
+            for (size_t kBegin = 0; kBegin < N; kBegin += innerBlock) {
+                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;
+                    for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
+                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 8.
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
+                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
+                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        }
+                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;
+                    for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
+                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 8.
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
+                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
+                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        }
+                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

 }
 
+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+            const double value = a[r * N + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                sums[r][j] += value * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+
 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
+    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
+    const double* a = A.data();
+    const double* b = B.data();
+    double* c = C.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];
+
+        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
+            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;

#define main benchmark_main
#include \"SOURCE\"

#include <random>
#include <iostream>
int main() {
    omp_set_dynamic(0);
    std::mt19937_64 random(42);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<double> values(-1.0, 1.0);
    double worst = 0;
    for (int threads : {1, 2, 4, 8}) {
        omp_set_num_threads(threads);
        for (size_t n : {0u, 1u, 2u, 3u, 4u, 5u, 7u, 8u, 9u, 15u, 16u, 17u,
                         31u, 32u, 33u, 63u, 64u, 65u, 95u, 127u, 128u, 129u, 257u}) {

            initMatrix(initial, n);
            for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) for(size_t j=0;j<n;++j)
                if(initial[i*n+j] "'!= getPseudoRndValue(n,i,j)) return 2;
            for (auto& v : a) v = values(random);
            for (auto& v : b) v = values(random);
            for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) for (size_t j=0;j<n;++j) {
                double sum = 0;
                for (size_t k=0;k<n;++k) sum += a[i*n+k]*b[k*n+j];
                ref[i*n+j] = sum;
            }
            for (int repeat=0;repeat<2;++repeat) {
                matrixMultiply(a,b,c,n);
                for(size_t i=0;i<c.size();++i) {
                    double error=std::abs(c[i]-ref[i])/std::max(1.0,std::abs(ref[i]));
                    worst=std::max(worst,error);
                    if (!std::isfinite(c[i]) || error > 1e-12) {
                        std::cerr << "Mismatch threads="<<threads<<" N="<<n<<" index="<<i<<" error="<<error<<"'"\\n\";
                        return 1;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

}

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+            const double value = a[r * N + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                sums[r][j] += value * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            const double value = packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+                sums[r][j] += a[r * N + k] * value;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+

                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {

+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
+    const double* a = A.data();
+    const double* b = B.data();
+    double* c = C.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];
+
+        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
+            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
+            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;
+
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
+                }
+            }

+            for (size_t kBegin = 0; kBegin < N; kBegin += innerBlock) {
+                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;

+                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
+                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 8.
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
+                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            const double value = packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+                sums[r][j] += a[r * N + k] * value;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+
 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
+    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
+    const double* a = A.data();
+    const double* b = B.data();
+    double* c = C.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];
+
+        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
+            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
+            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;
+
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+
+            for (size_t kBegin = 0; kBegin < N; kBegin += innerBlock) {
+                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;
+                    for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
+                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 8.
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
+                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
+                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        }
+                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Unrolling k would compete with the live output accumulators for SIMD
+    // registers; the independent columns already provide instruction parallelism.
+    #if defined(__GNUC__)
+    #pragma GCC unroll 1
+    #endif
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            const double value = packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+                sums[r][j] += a[r * N + k] * value;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+

+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
+    const double* a = A.data();

+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];

+        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
+            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
+            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;
+
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
+                }
+            }

+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd

+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+

+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;
+                    for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {

+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sum0[vectorColumns];
+    alignas(64) double sum1[vectorColumns];
+    alignas(64) double sum2[vectorColumns];
+    alignas(64) double sum3[vectorColumns];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+        sum0[j] = c[j];
+        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
+            sum1[j] = c[N + j];
+            sum2[j] = c[2 * N + j];
+            sum3[j] = c[3 * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
+            const double a1 = a[N + k];
+            const double a2 = a[2 * N + k];
+            const double a3 = a[3 * N + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                const double value = packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+                sum0[j] += a0 * value;
+                sum1[j] += a1 * value;
+                sum2[j] += a2 * value;
+                sum3[j] += a3 * value;
+            }
+        } else {
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                sum0[j] += a0 * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+        c[j] = sum0[j];
+        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
+            c[N + j] = sum1[j];
+            c[2 * N + j] = sum2[j];
+            c[3 * N + j] = sum3[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+
 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
+    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
+    const double* a = A.data();
+    const double* b = B.data();
+    double* c = C.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];
+
+        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
+            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
+            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;
+
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+
+            for (size_t kBegin = 0; kBegin < N; kBegin += innerBlock) {
+                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;
+                    for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
+                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 8.
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
+                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
+                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        }
+                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;
+
+// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sum0[vectorColumns];
+    alignas(64) double sum1[vectorColumns];
+    alignas(64) double sum2[vectorColumns];
+    alignas(64) double sum3[vectorColumns];
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+        sum0[j] = c[j];
+        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
+            sum1[j] = c[N + j];
+            sum2[j] = c[2 * N + j];
+            sum3[j] = c[3 * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        const double a0 = a[k];
+        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
+            const double a1 = a[N + k];
+            const double a2 = a[2 * N + k];
+            const double a3 = a[3 * N + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                const double value = packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+                sum0[j] += a0 * value;
+                sum1[j] += a1 * value;
+                sum2[j] += a2 * value;
+                sum3[j] += a3 * value;
+            }
+        } else {
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                sum0[j] += a0 * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+        c[j] = sum0[j];
+        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
+            c[N + j] = sum1[j];
+            c[2 * N + j] = sum2[j];
+            c[3 * N + j] = sum3[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+
 void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                     std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;

-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
+    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
+        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
+    const double* a = A.data();
+    const double* b = B.data();
+    double* c = C.data();
+
+    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
+    {
+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];
+
+        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
+            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
+            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;
+
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                #pragma omp simd
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
+                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
+                }
+            }
+
+            for (size_t kBegin = 0; kBegin < N; kBegin += innerBlock) {
+                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;
+                    for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
+                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 8.
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
+                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
+                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        }
+                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
+                    }
+                }
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

namespace {
constexpr size_t rowBlock = 32;
constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 8;

// Keep four rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
template <size_t Rows>
inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
    alignas(64) double sum0[vectorColumns];
    alignas(64) double sum1[vectorColumns];
    alignas(64) double sum2[vectorColumns];
    alignas(64) double sum3[vectorColumns];
    #pragma omp simd
    for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
        sum0[j] = c[j];
        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
            sum1[j] = c[N + j];
            sum2[j] = c[2 * N + j];
            sum3[j] = c[3 * N + j];
        }
    }

    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
        const double a0 = a[k];
        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
            const double a1 = a[N + k];
            const double a2 = a[2 * N + k];
            const double a3 = a[3 * N + k];
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
                const double value = packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
                sum0[j] += a0 * value;
                sum1[j] += a1 * value;
                sum2[j] += a2 * value;
                sum3[j] += a3 * value;
            }
        } else {
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
                sum0[j] += a0 * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
            }
        }
    }

    #pragma omp simd
    for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
        c[j] = sum0[j];
        if constexpr (Rows == 4) {
            c[N + j] = sum1[j];
            c[2 * N + j] = sum2[j];
            c[3 * N + j] = sum3[j];
        }
    }
}
} // namespace

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
    const int threads = static_cast<int>(std::min(
        static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()), std::max(size_t{1}, tileCount)));
    const double* a = A.data();
    const double* b = B.data();
    double* c = C.data();

    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
    {
        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
        // the innermost loop and lets four rows reuse each loaded B vector.
        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];

        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;

            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
                }
            }

            for (size_t kBegin = 0; kBegin < N; kBegin += innerBlock) {
                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                        #pragma omp simd
                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
                        }
                    }
                }

                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
                    size_t i = 0;
                    for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {
                        multiplyMicroTile<4>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
                                             N, depth);
                    }
                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
                                             N, depth);
                    }
                }

                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 8.
                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
                        }
                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t microRows = 2;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 16;
+
+int matrixThreadCount(const size_t N) {
+    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
+    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
+    // Reuse the same team for initialization and multiplication, and avoid
+    // creating idle workers when there are fewer output tiles than threads.
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+        std::max(size_t{1}, rowTiles * columnTiles)));
+}
+
+// Keep two rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+            const double value = a[r * N + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                sums[r][j] += value * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+

+    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
+    const int threads = matrixThreadCount(N);
+    const double* a = A.data();
+    const double* b = B.data();

+    {
+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets multiple rows reuse each packed B panel.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];

+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)

+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;

+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;

+                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }

+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;

+                        multiplyMicroTile<microRows>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                                    panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,

#define main benchmark_main
#include \"SOURCE\"
#undef main
#include <random>
#include <iostream>
int main() {
    omp_set_dynamic(0);
    std::mt19937_64 random(42);
    std::uniform_real_distribution<double> values(-1.0, 1.0);
    double worst = 0;
    for (size_t n : {0u, 1u, 2u, 3u, 4u, 5u, 7u, 8u, 9u, 15u, 16u, 17u,
                     31u, 32u, 33u, 63u, 64u, 65u, 95u, 127u, 128u, 129u,
                     255u, 256u, 257u, 512u}) {
        std::vector<double> a(n*n), b(n*n), c(n*n, -123), ref(n*n), first, initial(n*n);
        for (auto& v : a) v = values(random);
        for (auto& v : b) v = values(random);
        for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) for (size_t j=0;j<n;++j) {
            double sum = 0;
            for (size_t k=0;k<n;++k) sum += a[i*n+k]*b[k*n+j];
            ref[i*n+j] = sum;
        }
        for (int threads : {1, 2, 4, 8, 16}) {
            omp_set_num_threads(threads);
            initMatrix(initial, n);
            for (size_t i=0;i<n;++i) for(size_t j=0;j<n;++j)
                if(initial[i*n+j] "'!= getPseudoRndValue(n,i,j)) return 2;
            for (int repeat=0;repeat<2;++repeat) {
                matrixMultiply(a,b,c,n);
                for(size_t i=0;i<c.size();++i) {
                    double error=std::abs(c[i]-ref[i])/std::max(1.0,std::abs(ref[i]));
                    worst=std::max(worst,error);
                    if (!std::isfinite(c[i]) || error > 1e-12) {
                        std::cerr << "Mismatch threads="<<threads<<" N="<<n<<" index="<<i<<" error="<<error<<"'"\\n\";
                        return 1;
                    }

                else if (c"'!=first) {
                    std::cerr << "Thread-dependent result at N="<<n<<"'"\\n\";
                    return 3;
                }
            }
        }
    }

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(matmul_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <omp.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

namespace {
constexpr size_t rowBlock = 128;
constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
constexpr size_t microRows = 2;
constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 16;

int matrixThreadCount(const size_t N) {
    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
    // Reuse the same team for initialization and multiplication, and avoid
    // creating idle workers when there are fewer output tiles than threads.
    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
        std::max(size_t{1}, rowTiles * columnTiles)));
}

// Keep two rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
template <size_t Rows>
inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
        }
    }

    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
        for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
            const double value = a[r * N + k];
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
                sums[r][j] += value * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
            }
        }
    }

    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
        #pragma omp simd
        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
        }
    }
}
} // namespace

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    const int threads = matrixThreadCount(N);
    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(threads) schedule(static)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
    const size_t tileCount = rowTiles * columnTiles;
    const int threads = matrixThreadCount(N);
    const double* a = A.data();
    const double* b = B.data();
    double* c = C.data();

    #pragma omp parallel num_threads(threads)
    {
        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
        // the innermost loop and lets multiple rows reuse each packed B panel.
        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];

        // A worker owns an entire output tile, including all k blocks. There
        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
        #pragma omp for schedule(static)
        for (size_t tile = 0; tile < tileCount; ++tile) {
            const size_t iBegin = (tile / columnTiles) * rowBlock;
            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;

            for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
                #pragma omp simd
                for (size_t j = 0; j < columns; ++j) {
                    c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = 0.0;
                }
            }

            for (size_t kBegin = 0; kBegin < N; kBegin += innerBlock) {
                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                        #pragma omp simd
                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
                        }
                    }
                }

                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
                    size_t i = 0;
                    for (; i + microRows <= rows; i += microRows) {
                        multiplyMicroTile<microRows>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
                                                    panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
                                                    N, depth);
                    }
                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
                                             N, depth);
                    }
                }

                // Handle an incomplete SIMD tile without touching padding or
                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 16.
                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
                        }
                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / seconds / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+namespace {
+constexpr size_t rowBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t columnBlock = 64;
+constexpr size_t innerBlock = 128;
+constexpr size_t microRows = 2;
+constexpr size_t vectorColumns = 16;
+
+int matrixThreadCount(const size_t N) {
+    const size_t rowTiles = N / rowBlock + (N % rowBlock != 0);
+    const size_t columnTiles = N / columnBlock + (N % columnBlock != 0);
+    // Reuse the same team for initialization and multiplication, and avoid
+    // creating idle workers when there are fewer output tiles than threads.
+    return static_cast<int>(std::min(static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads()),
+        std::max(size_t{1}, rowTiles * columnTiles)));
+}
+
+// Keep two rows of independent accumulators in SIMD registers. Only columns
+// are vectorized: each element's products are added in increasing k order.
+template <size_t Rows>
+inline void multiplyMicroTile(const double* a, const double* packedB,
+                              double* c, const size_t N, const size_t depth) {
+    alignas(64) double sums[Rows][vectorColumns];
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            sums[r][j] = c[r * N + j];
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+        for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+            const double value = a[r * N + k];
+            #pragma omp simd
+            for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+                sums[r][j] += value * packedB[k * vectorColumns + j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    for (size_t r = 0; r < Rows; ++r) {
+        #pragma omp simd
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < vectorColumns; ++j) {
+            c[r * N + j] = sums[r][j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+} // namespace
+

+    const int threads = matrixThreadCount(N);
+    #pragma omp parallel for num_threads(threads) schedule(static)

+    const int threads = matrixThreadCount(N);
+    const double* a = A.data();

+        // Private, bounded scratch space avoids allocation and synchronization
+        // inside the multiplication. Packing removes B's long row stride from
+        // the innermost loop and lets multiple rows reuse each packed B panel.
+        alignas(64) double packedB[innerBlock * columnBlock];

+        // are no shared accumulators, atomics, or floating-point reductions.
+        #pragma omp for schedule(static)

+            const size_t jBegin = (tile % columnTiles) * columnBlock;
+            const size_t rows = std::min(rowBlock, N - iBegin);
+            const size_t columns = std::min(columnBlock, N - jBegin);
+            const size_t fullColumns = columns - columns % vectorColumns;

+                }

+                const size_t depth = std::min(innerBlock, N - kBegin);
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                        #pragma omp simd
+                        for (size_t lane = 0; lane < vectorColumns; ++lane) {
+                            panel[k * vectorColumns + lane] =
+                                b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j + lane];
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t j = 0; j < fullColumns; j += vectorColumns) {
+                    const double* panel = packedB + j * innerBlock;
+                    size_t i = 0;
+                    for (; i + microRows <= rows; i += microRows) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<microRows>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                                    panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                                    N, depth);
+                    }
+                    for (; i < rows; ++i) {
+                        multiplyMicroTile<1>(a + (iBegin + i) * N + kBegin,
+                                             panel, c + (iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j,
+                                             N, depth);

+                }

+                // adding extra products, including for matrices smaller than 16.
+                for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {
+                    for (size_t j = fullColumns; j < columns; ++j) {
+                        double sum = c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        for (size_t k = 0; k < depth; ++k) {
+                            sum += a[(iBegin + i) * N + kBegin + k] *
+                                   b[(kBegin + k) * N + jBegin + j];
+                        }
+                        c[(iBegin + i) * N + jBegin + j] = sum;
+                    }
